        Idea:
# Sprint brief — Tickets MVP

## Goal

Implement the tickets feature in megaplan per the design in `docs/tickets.md`. Tickets are short, repo-scoped notes on issues/problems, captured by humans or by agents on a human's prompt, that get folded into epics and auto-addressed when the resolving epic completes.

**The design doc `docs/tickets.md` is the source of truth for the data model, CLI surface, file format, modes, codebase identity, and integration touchpoints. Read it first. This brief only adds sprint scoping — what's in, what's out, what success looks like.**

## In scope for this sprint

Everything required to make tickets usable end-to-end in both **local-only mode** and **cloud-configured mode**:

1. **Schema migration (cloud-only)**: add `tickets` and `ticket_epics` tables; add `root_commit_sha` column to `codebases` with an index. Additive only — no changes to existing tables beyond the new column.
2. **`megaplan/store/db.py`**: CRUD helpers for the new tables, column allowlist entries, codebase resolution that prefers `root_commit_sha`.
3. **New module `megaplan/tickets/`**: the canonical local representation. Frontmatter read/write for `.megaplan/tickets/{ulid}-{slug}.md` files. Mode-aware dispatch (cloud-configured → file + DB; local-only → file only). Codebase identity resolution from cwd via `git rev-list --max-parents=0 HEAD`.
4. **CLI `megaplan ticket …`** with all verbs in `docs/tickets.md` § CLI: `new`, `list`, `show`, `edit`, `link`, `unlink`, `addressed`, `dismiss`, `reopen`. `ticket new` prints only the ULID to stdout on success; `--json` on read commands. `source` auto-derived from `MEGAPLAN_TURN_ID` env.
5. **Auto-address hook**: in the epic state-transition path, after an epic moves to `done`, call `megaplan.tickets.address_resolved_by_epic(epic_id)`. Implementation handles both modes per the design doc. Idempotent.
6. **Planner discovery integration**: when assembling context for new/refined epics, include open tickets for the current repo, ranked by `tag overlap with epic goal desc, created_at desc`. The planner can propose links with `resolves_on_complete=true`. Same data shape in both modes (DB query in cloud, file scan in local-only).
7. **Resident/agent prompt**: a short paragraph teaching the agent that `megaplan ticket new` exists and when to use it (out-of-scope observations during epic work, user-requested captures).
8. **Tests** covering both modes for: file ↔ DB round-trip, link/unlink, auto-address idempotence, codebase identity resolution (rename case, fresh-repo case), CLI happy paths.

## Out of scope (defer)

- **Offline queue / `.pending/` directory / `megaplan ticket sync`**. Write-through only for now; if the DB write fails in cloud mode, the CLI errors loudly and the user retries. The design doc describes the offline queue — explicitly **do not build it** this sprint. Build the minimal write-through path.
- Conflict detection on concurrent edits — last-write-wins is fine.
- Severity, assignment, comments, cross-repo tickets — explicitly excluded per the design doc.
- Anything described in the doc's **Open items** section.

## Success criteria

- `megaplan ticket new "title" -b "body"` works in a local-only repo and writes the `.md` file.
- The same command in a cloud-configured repo writes the `.md` file **and** inserts a `tickets` row, auto-registering the codebase if needed (with `root_commit_sha` populated).
- `megaplan ticket link <ticket> <epic> --resolves` updates both the file frontmatter and the DB join row.
- Marking an epic `done` flips the linked ticket's status to `addressed` and writes a `resolution_note` referencing the epic — in both modes.
- New epic creation surfaces open tickets for the repo in the planner's context, ranked.
- Tests pass for both modes.

## Notes for the planner

- Follow the **core-first principle**: `megaplan/tickets/` owns the capability; the DB layer is purely additive and only kicks in when a store is configured. Mode detection follows whatever mechanism megaplan core already uses (presence of a configured store) — do not invent a new one.
- The `tickets` concept is **distinct from `feedback`**. Do not reuse the `feedback` table; do not conflate their lifecycles.
- Vendor flags / FK conventions / migration patterns should match how existing tables are handled in `megaplan/store/db.py`. Read existing CRUD helpers before designing new ones.
- The auto-address hook integration point is the one place where you must touch existing epic state-transition code. Find the existing hook surface (where epic events are emitted on state change) and add the call there — don't invent a parallel transition path.

```
1	# Tickets
2	
3	A lightweight way to capture problems, bugs, or "we should look at this" notes against a repo, so they can later be folded into epics. Tickets are deliberately small: just enough structure to triage, link, and auto-close when the work that addresses them ships.
4	
5	## Why this is a new concept (not `feedback`)
6	
7	The existing `feedback` table holds guidance about *the tooling itself* — corrections to how the agent behaves, what to avoid, what worked. It is injected into prompts.
8	
9	Tickets are different: they describe *a problem in a codebase* that may become work. They are inert until folded into an epic. Keep the tables separate; no shared lifecycle.
10	
11	## Modes
12	
13	Megaplan runs in two modes; tickets must work in both.
14	
15	- **Local-only** — no cloud configured. The `.md` files on disk are the sole source of truth. No DB row, no codebase row. Identity is the repo's root commit SHA, computed on demand.
16	- **Cloud-configured** — DB store available. The `.md` files remain on disk and committed to the repo, and a mirrored row exists in `tickets` / `ticket_epics`. The DB is the system of record for live state; the file is the human-readable artifact.
17	
18	Mode detection follows the same rule megaplan core already uses (presence of a configured store). The CLI dispatches: if cloud is configured, write-through to DB and sync; otherwise file-only operations.
19	
20	Everything in the rest of this doc applies to both modes unless explicitly marked **(cloud-only)**.
21	
22	## Data model
23	
24	The tables below are **cloud-only**: they're created and used when a store is configured. In local-only mode, the equivalent state lives entirely in `.md` frontmatter (see [Local format](#local-format)).
25	
26	### New table: `tickets` (cloud-only)
27	
28	| column | type | notes |
29	|---|---|---|
30	| `id` | ULID | also the local filename stem |
31	| `codebase_id` | FK → `codebases.id` | required, primary scoping |
32	| `title` | text | required, ≤120 chars, one line |
33	| `body` | text | required, markdown, non-empty |
34	| `tags` | text[] | optional, freeform |
35	| `status` | enum | `open` \| `addressed` \| `dismissed`, default `open` |
36	| `source` | enum | `human` \| `agent` \| `post-mortem` |
37	| `filed_by_actor_id` | FK → actors | nullable |
38	| `filed_in_turn_id` | FK → `bot_turns` | nullable; populated when agent files mid-turn |
39	| `resolution_note` | text | filled on addressed/dismissed |
40	| `created_at`, `last_edited_at`, `addressed_at`, `dismissed_at` | timestamps | |
41	
42	### New table: `ticket_epics` (many-to-many, cloud-only)
43	
44	| column | type | notes |
45	|---|---|---|
46	| `ticket_id` | FK | |
47	| `epic_id` | FK | |
48	| `resolves_on_complete` | bool | **the key field** — does completing this epic close this ticket? |
49	| `linked_at`, `linked_by_actor_id`, `note` | | |
50	
51	Primary key `(ticket_id, epic_id)`. A ticket can be linked to N epics over its life; an epic can address N tickets.
52	
53	### Addition to `codebases` (cloud-only)
54	
55	Add `root_commit_sha` (text, indexed). Used as the durable repo identity — see [Codebase identity](#codebase-identity). Backfilled lazily for existing rows on first access. In local-only mode this column doesn't apply; the root SHA is computed directly from `git` when needed.
56	
57	That's the entire schema change. No new event/audit tables — provenance is covered by `filed_by_actor_id` / `filed_in_turn_id` and the local file's git history.
58	
59	## Local format
60	
61	One markdown file per ticket, **committed to the repo**:
62	
63	```
64	<repo>/.megaplan/tickets/{ulid}-{slug}.md
65	```
66	
67	The path establishes the repo association — no `codebase_id` in frontmatter.
68	
69	```markdown
70	---
71	id: 01HXY...
72	title: Execute step retries swallow stderr on timeout
73	tags: [execute, observability]
74	status: open
75	source: agent
76	filed_by: actor_01H...        # raw string; FK only resolved in cloud mode
77	filed_in_turn: turn_01H...    # raw string; FK only resolved in cloud mode
78	created_at: 2026-05-11T14:22:00Z
79	epics:
80	  - id: epic_01HZ...
81	    resolves_on_complete: true
82	---
83	
84	# Body
85	
86	Markdown prose describing the issue.
87	```
88	
89	Frontmatter is **complete in local-only mode** — it carries everything needed to reconstruct ticket state without a DB. In cloud mode, the same fields are mirrored to the `tickets` row; `filed_by` / `filed_in_turn` become real FKs (`filed_by_actor_id`, `filed_in_turn_id`). The `epics:` list mirrors the `ticket_epics` join in cloud mode and is the sole representation of links in local-only mode.
90	
91	## CLI
92	
93	One surface. The agent shells out to it; no separate tool API.
94	
95	```
96	megaplan ticket new "<title>" [-b "<body>" | --edit | -]  [-t tag1,tag2]
97	megaplan ticket list   [--tag X] [--status open] [--epic E]
98	megaplan ticket show   <id>
99	megaplan ticket edit   <id>                              # opens $EDITOR on the .md
100	megaplan ticket link   <ticket> <epic> [--resolves]
101	megaplan ticket unlink <ticket> <epic>
102	megaplan ticket addressed <id> [--note "..."]
103	megaplan ticket dismiss   <id> [--note "..."]
104	megaplan ticket reopen    <id>
105	```
106	
107	Conventions:
108	
109	- `--json` on any read command emits structured output.
110	- `ticket new` prints **only the ULID** to stdout on success (human chatter to stderr) so the agent can pipe/capture it trivially.
111	- `source` is auto-derived: if `MEGAPLAN_TURN_ID` is set in env, `source=agent` and `filed_by` / `filed_in_turn` are populated; otherwise `source=human`.
112	- All commands resolve the repo from cwd (see [Codebase identity](#codebase-identity)). In cloud mode this resolves to a `codebase_id` (auto-registering the row if needed). In local-only mode it's just the cwd repo; no registration step.
113	- `list` scope differs by mode: in local-only it lists files under `<cwd>/.megaplan/tickets/`. In cloud mode the same command can also accept `--codebase <owner>/<name>` to query across repos via the DB; without that flag it defaults to the current repo.
114	
115	## Lifecycle & auto-addressing
116	
117	1. **File** → `status=open`, written to `.md` and DB.
118	2. **Link to epic** → join row created, optionally `resolves_on_complete=true`.
119	3. **Epic completes** → for each ticket linked with `resolves_on_complete=true`, flip ticket to `addressed`, set `addressed_at`, fill `resolution_note` with `"Resolved by epic <id> on <date>: <epic title>"`. Idempotent — re-running on an already-addressed ticket is a no-op.
120	4. **Epic cancelled/dismissed** → ticket status is untouched. Link stays as history.
121	5. **Reopen** → `reopen` flips ticket back to `open`. The linking epic's join row has `resolves_on_complete` demoted to `false` and a note appended.
122	
123	Auto-addressing fires from the epic state-transition hook (same place that emits epic events). Add a single call after `state → done`: `address_tickets_resolved_by_epic(epic_id)`. The implementation has two paths, dispatched on mode:
124	
125	- **Cloud-configured** — updates `tickets` rows and the corresponding `.md` frontmatter in the working repo (if present).
126	- **Local-only** — walks `.megaplan/tickets/*.md`, finds files whose `epics:` list contains this epic with `resolves_on_complete: true`, and updates their frontmatter in place.
127	
128	Both paths are idempotent.
129	
130	## Discovery at planning time
131	
132	When a new epic is created or refined for a repo, surface the open tickets for that codebase (ranked by tag overlap with the epic goal + recency) into the planner prompt. The planner proposes which to link with `resolves_on_complete=true`. This is the natural "folding" moment — no separate ceremony.
133	
134	A standalone `megaplan ticket link` exists for ad-hoc linking outside that flow.
135	
136	## Codebase identity
137	
138	Durable key: the SHA of the repo's root commit (`git rev-list --max-parents=0 HEAD`). Survives rename, transfer, fork, remote URL change.
139	
140	**Local-only mode**: compute the root SHA on demand. That's the identity. No row, no registration.
141	
142	**Cloud-configured mode**: stored as `codebases.root_commit_sha`. Resolution order on any ticket CLI invocation:
143	
144	1. Compute the root SHA from cwd.
145	2. Look up codebase by `root_commit_sha`. If found, use that `codebase_id`. If `(owner, name)` no longer matches the current remote, silently update those columns — the ID is preserved, all tickets keep working.
146	3. If not found, fall back to `(owner, name)` lookup (handles pre-existing rows without `root_commit_sha` — backfill it on hit).
147	4. If still not found, auto-register a new codebase row using current owner/name/remote and the root SHA, then proceed.
148	
149	Edge cases:
150	
151	- **No commits yet** → CLI refuses; tell the user to make their first commit.
152	- **Multiple root commits** (rare) → use the smallest SHA when sorted; document.
153	- **Submodule / subdir** → root SHA is the outer repo's. Tickets attach to the repo, not the subdir.
154	
155	## Sync model
156	
157	Mode-dependent:
158	
159	- **Local-only** — every operation writes the `.md` file. That's it. No queue, no sync, no DB.
160	- **Cloud-configured** — write-through with an offline fallback queue:
161	  - Every `ticket new/edit/link/...` writes the `.md` and pushes to DB in the same call.
162	  - If the DB write fails (offline, network error), the intent is stashed in `.megaplan/tickets/.pending/{ulid}.json`.
163	  - The next successful online command (or an explicit `megaplan ticket sync`) flushes the pending queue.
164	  - The `.md` files are the truth offline; the DB is the truth when reachable. Reconcile on flush by comparing `last_edited_at`.
165	
166	`megaplan ticket sync` is a no-op in local-only mode.
167	
168	## What this does NOT include (intentionally)
169	
170	- No severity field — tags cover prioritization for now.
171	- No assignment / owner — tickets are repo-scoped, not person-scoped.
172	- No comments / threads — edit the body or add a new ticket.
173	- No cross-repo tickets — one ticket, one codebase. Use a `cross-repo` tag if needed.
174	- No separate audit table — provenance is on the row + git history of the `.md`.
175	- No agent-facing tool API distinct from the CLI.
176	
177	If any of these become real pain points, revisit. Don't pre-build.
178	
179	## Integration touchpoints (what to change beyond the new tables)
180	
181	Follow the core-first principle: the local-file path lives in `_core` / `megaplan/tickets/`; DB persistence is layered on top and only kicks in when a store is configured.
182	
183	- New module `megaplan/tickets/` — the canonical local representation (read/write `.md` files, frontmatter parsing, link/address operations on files), plus mode-aware dispatch into the store. This module is what the CLI and the epic hook both call.
184	- `megaplan/store/db.py` **(cloud-only)**: add `tickets` and `ticket_epics` to the column allowlists; CRUD helpers (`create_ticket`, `list_tickets`, `link_ticket_to_epic`, `address_tickets_resolved_by_epic`, etc.); add `root_commit_sha` to `codebases` allowlist.
185	- Schema migration **(cloud-only)**: create the two tables; `ALTER TABLE codebases ADD COLUMN root_commit_sha text` with an index.
186	- `megaplan/cli.py`: register the `ticket` subcommand group. CLI handlers call into `megaplan/tickets/`, which internally decides whether to also touch the store.
187	- Epic state-transition hook (wherever an epic moves to `done`): call `megaplan.tickets.address_resolved_by_epic(epic_id)`. The function runs the file-update path in both modes, and the DB-update path additionally in cloud mode.
188	- Epic planner prompt assembly: when building context for a new/refined epic, include open tickets for the repo. In local-only mode this reads `.megaplan/tickets/*.md`; in cloud mode it queries the DB. Same shape of data, different source.
189	- Resident/agent prompt: a short paragraph telling the agent that `megaplan ticket new` exists and when to reach for it (out-of-scope observations during epic work, user-requested captures).
190	
191	## Open items
192	
193	- Exact ranking heuristic for "open tickets shown at planning time" — start with `tag overlap desc, created_at desc`, refine if it's noisy.
194	- Whether `ticket edit` should diff the new frontmatter against the DB and reject conflicting concurrent edits, or last-write-wins. Last-write-wins for now; add conflict detection only if it bites.
195
```

{
  "plan": "## Implementation Plan: Tickets MVP\n\n## Overview\nAdd a repo-scoped tickets feature per `docs/tickets.md`. Build a new `megaplan/tickets/` module that owns the canonical local representation (`.megaplan/tickets/{ulid}-{slug}.md` with YAML frontmatter), wire mode-aware dispatch into the existing DB store, register a new `megaplan ticket …` CLI subcommand group, and inject one auto-address call into the existing epic state-transition path (`megaplan/store/db.py:960` and `megaplan/store/file.py:1054`). DB changes are additive: new `tickets` and `ticket_epics` tables plus a `root_commit_sha` column on `codebases`. Local-only mode operates entirely on files; cloud-configured mode write-throughs to DB. **No offline `.pending/` queue this sprint** — DB write failure raises loudly.\n\nExisting repo shape:\n- Migrations live in `supabase/migrations/` (latest: `202605060001_resident_orchestration.sql`). New file should follow naming `2026MMDDHHMM_*.sql`.\n- DB store: `megaplan/store/db.py` with column allowlists at line ~237; codebase CRUD already exists (`create_codebase`, `find_codebase`, `update_codebase`, lines 1779–1955).\n- CLI: subparsers added in `megaplan/cli.py:1176+`.\n- Epic completion sites: `megaplan/store/db.py:960` (DB) and `megaplan/store/file.py:1054` (file backend) — both gate on `changes.get(\"status\") == \"done\"`. This is the auto-address hook surface.\n- Planner context assembly: `megaplan/handlers/plan.py` (and supporting `megaplan/_core/`).\n\n## Main Phase\n\n### Step 1: Audit existing patterns (`megaplan/store/db.py`, `megaplan/store/file.py`, `megaplan/cli.py`, `megaplan/handlers/plan.py`)\n**Scope:** Small\n1. **Read** the codebase CRUD block (`megaplan/store/db.py:1779-1955`) and column allowlists (`megaplan/store/db.py:237-238`) to mirror naming/return conventions for the new `tickets` / `ticket_epics` helpers.\n2. **Read** the epic→done branches at `megaplan/store/db.py:960` and `megaplan/store/file.py:1054` — these are the two call sites for the auto-address hook (one per backend).\n3. **Skim** `megaplan/cli.py` ~line 1256 (`epic` subparser) for the established subparser pattern; mirror it for `ticket`.\n4. **Locate** where planner context for a new/refined epic is assembled (`megaplan/handlers/plan.py`) and identify the insertion point for \"open tickets for repo\".\n\n### Step 2: Schema migration (`supabase/migrations/2026MMDDHHMM_tickets.sql`)\n**Scope:** Small\n1. **Create** a new migration file, additive only:\n   - `ALTER TABLE codebases ADD COLUMN root_commit_sha text;` + `CREATE INDEX idx_codebases_root_commit_sha ON codebases(root_commit_sha);`\n   - `CREATE TABLE tickets (id text PRIMARY KEY, codebase_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES codebases(id), title text NOT NULL, body text NOT NULL, tags text[] DEFAULT '{}', status text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'open' CHECK (status IN ('open','addressed','dismissed')), source text NOT NULL CHECK (source IN ('human','agent','post-mortem')), filed_by_actor_id text REFERENCES … , filed_in_turn_id text REFERENCES bot_turns(id), resolution_note text, created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(), last_edited_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(), addressed_at timestamptz, dismissed_at timestamptz);` plus an index on `(codebase_id, status)`.\n   - `CREATE TABLE ticket_epics (ticket_id text REFERENCES tickets(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, epic_id text REFERENCES … , resolves_on_complete boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false, linked_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(), linked_by_actor_id text, note text, PRIMARY KEY (ticket_id, epic_id));` plus an index on `epic_id`.\n2. **Match** the FK style (uuid vs text, ON DELETE behavior, `now()` defaults) used by the latest existing migrations — verify by reading `202604300009_009_codebase_research.sql` or similar for actor / bot_turn FK column types.\n\n### Step 3: Schema dataclasses (`megaplan/schemas/models.py` or `megaplan/schemas/sprint1.py`)\n**Scope:** Small\n1. **Add** `Ticket` and `TicketEpicLink` Pydantic/dataclass models matching the new tables; export from `megaplan/schemas/__init__.py`.\n2. **Add** `root_commit_sha: str | None` to the existing `Codebase` model.\n\n### Step 4: DB store CRUD (`megaplan/store/db.py`)\n**Scope:** Medium\n1. **Add** to the `_IDEMPOTENT_MUTATORS` set and module exports: `create_ticket`, `update_ticket`, `link_ticket_to_epic`, `unlink_ticket_from_epic`, `address_tickets_resolved_by_epic`.\n2. **Extend** the column allowlists (`megaplan/store/db.py:237`): add entries for `tickets` and `ticket_epics`; add `root_commit_sha` to the `codebases` allowlist.\n3. **Implement** CRUD methods modeled on the codebase block (`megaplan/store/db.py:1779-1955`):\n   - `create_ticket(...)`, `load_ticket(id)`, `list_tickets(codebase_id=…, status=…, tag=…, epic_id=…)`, `update_ticket(id, **changes)`.\n   - `link_ticket_to_epic(ticket_id, epic_id, resolves_on_complete, note=None)`, `unlink_ticket_from_epic(...)`, `list_ticket_epic_links(ticket_id=…|epic_id=…)`.\n   - `address_tickets_resolved_by_epic(epic_id) -> list[str]`: single SQL update setting `status='addressed'`, `addressed_at=now()`, `resolution_note='Resolved by epic <epic_id> on <date>: <title>'` for tickets joined via `ticket_epics` with `resolves_on_complete=true` and current `status='open'`. Returns affected ticket IDs. Idempotent.\n4. **Add** codebase resolution helper `resolve_codebase_by_root_sha(root_sha, owner, name, repo_url) -> codebase_id`: lookup by `root_commit_sha` first, fall back to `(owner,name)` and backfill `root_commit_sha`, else `create_codebase` with all four populated.\n\n### Step 5: New module `megaplan/tickets/` (canonical local representation)\n**Scope:** Medium\n1. **Create** `megaplan/tickets/__init__.py` exporting the public API: `new`, `list_open`, `show`, `edit`, `link`, `unlink`, `mark_addressed`, `mark_dismissed`, `reopen`, `address_resolved_by_epic`.\n2. **Create** `megaplan/tickets/identity.py`:\n   - `repo_root_sha(cwd) -> str` via `git rev-list --max-parents=0 HEAD`; raises a clean error if there are no commits; if multiple roots, sorts and picks smallest.\n   - `repo_owner_name(cwd) -> tuple[str|None, str]` from `git remote get-url origin` (best-effort; tolerate missing remote in local-only mode).\n3. **Create** `megaplan/tickets/files.py`:\n   - `tickets_dir(repo_root) -> Path` returning `<repo>/.megaplan/tickets/`.\n   - `read_ticket_file(path) -> TicketDoc` and `write_ticket_file(path, doc)` using a YAML frontmatter parser (use stdlib + a minimal YAML — check if `pyyaml` is already a dep; otherwise use the project's existing frontmatter helper if any in `megaplan/_core/io.py`).\n   - `slugify(title)` for filename stem; full path `{ulid}-{slug}.md`.\n   - `iter_ticket_files(repo_root)` and `find_ticket_file(repo_root, ulid)`.\n4. **Create** `megaplan/tickets/core.py` — mode-aware dispatch:\n   - `_get_store()` returns the configured store (mirror whatever `_core` already does for store detection — do not invent a new mechanism).\n   - Each public op: always touches the file; if a store is configured, also calls the corresponding DB helper. On DB error in cloud mode: raise loudly (no `.pending/` queue this sprint).\n   - `address_resolved_by_epic(epic_id, *, store=None, repo_root=None)`: file-update path always (walk `.megaplan/tickets/*.md`, flip frontmatter `status` to `addressed` for any file whose `epics:` list contains `epic_id` with `resolves_on_complete: true`); cloud path additionally calls `store.address_tickets_resolved_by_epic(epic_id)`. Idempotent: skip files whose status is already `addressed` or `dismissed`.\n5. **Source derivation**: helper that reads `MEGAPLAN_TURN_ID` env; if set, populates `source='agent'` + `filed_in_turn`; else `source='human'`.\n\n### Step 6: CLI subcommand group (`megaplan/cli.py`, `megaplan/handlers/tickets.py`)\n**Scope:** Medium\n1. **Register** a new `ticket` subparser in `megaplan/cli.py` (mirror the `epic` block at ~line 1256) with verbs: `new`, `list`, `show`, `edit`, `link`, `unlink`, `addressed`, `dismiss`, `reopen`. Add `--json` to read commands, `--tag/--status/--epic` filters to `list`, `--resolves` to `link`, `--note` to `addressed/dismiss`, `-b/--edit/-` to `new`.\n2. **Create** `megaplan/handlers/tickets.py` with one handler per verb that calls into `megaplan.tickets` and renders output.\n   - `ticket new`: writes only the ULID to stdout; everything else to stderr.\n   - `ticket list`: local-only walks files in cwd; cloud mode queries DB (accepts `--codebase owner/name`).\n3. **Wire** dispatch in the main `cli.py` command router.\n\n### Step 7: Auto-address hook (`megaplan/store/db.py:960`, `megaplan/store/file.py:1054`)\n**Scope:** Small\n1. **In** `megaplan/store/db.py` `update_epic` — after the `status == \"done\"` branch sets `completed_at`, after the row is persisted, call `megaplan.tickets.address_resolved_by_epic(epic_id, store=self)`. Wrap import locally to avoid circular imports.\n2. **Mirror** the same call in `megaplan/store/file.py:1054`.\n3. **Idempotency** comes from the helper itself; no extra guards needed here.\n\n### Step 8: Planner discovery integration (`megaplan/handlers/plan.py` + planner prompt assembly)\n**Scope:** Small\n1. **Identify** the function that builds context for a new/refined epic (in `megaplan/handlers/plan.py` or `megaplan/prompts/`).\n2. **Add** a section \"Open tickets for this repo\" listing the top N (e.g., 20) open tickets ranked by `tag overlap with epic goal/title desc, created_at desc`. Source: cloud mode → `store.list_tickets(codebase_id=…, status='open')`; local-only → `megaplan.tickets.list_open(repo_root)`.\n3. **Mention** in the prompt that the planner can propose links with `resolves_on_complete=true` (the actual linking still happens via `ticket link` after epic creation).\n\n### Step 9: Resident/agent prompt (`megaplan/data/instructions.md` or `megaplan/resident/profile.py`)\n**Scope:** Small\n1. **Add** a short paragraph to the resident agent instructions: `megaplan ticket new \"<title>\" -b \"<body>\"` exists; use it to capture out-of-scope observations during epic work or to record user-requested bugs/notes. ULID is printed to stdout.\n\n### Step 10: Tests (`tests/test_tickets.py`, extend `tests/test_cli_entry.py`)\n**Scope:** Medium\n1. **Local-only mode**: tmp git repo, no store configured. Cover `new` writes the file with correct frontmatter; `list` finds it; `link`/`unlink` updates frontmatter `epics:`; `addressed`/`reopen`/`dismiss` flip `status`; `address_resolved_by_epic` walks files and updates idempotently.\n2. **Cloud-configured mode**: with an in-memory/sqlite-backed `DBStore` fixture (mirror existing `tests/conftest.py` patterns). Cover the same ops doing file + DB write-through; auto-register codebase on first call with `root_commit_sha`; `link` writes both file frontmatter and `ticket_epics` row.\n3. **Auto-address**: epic transitions to `done` via `update_epic`; verify linked tickets flip to `addressed` in both file and DB; second call is a no-op.\n4. **Codebase identity**: rename case (owner/name changes but `root_commit_sha` stable → same `codebase_id`); fresh-repo case (no commits → CLI errors cleanly).\n5. **CLI happy paths**: `megaplan ticket new`, `list --json`, `link --resolves` round-trip via `subprocess` or the existing CLI test harness.\n\n### Step 11: Validation\n**Scope:** Small\n1. **Run** the new test file first: `pytest tests/test_tickets.py -x`.\n2. **Run** the broader suite focused on touched areas: `pytest tests/test_cli_entry.py tests/test_auto.py -x`.\n3. **Run** the full suite: `pytest -x`.\n4. **Smoke**: in a scratch git repo, `MEGAPLAN_TURN_ID=t_test megaplan ticket new \"test\" -b \"body\" -t demo`; verify stdout is just the ULID, file exists with `source: agent`, `filed_in_turn: t_test`.\n\n## Execution Order\n1.         Idea:
# Sprint brief — Tickets MVP

## Goal

Implement the tickets feature in megaplan per the design in `docs/tickets.md`. Tickets are short, repo-scoped notes on issues/problems, captured by humans or by agents on a human's prompt, that get folded into epics and auto-addressed when the resolving epic completes.

**The design doc `docs/tickets.md` is the source of truth for the data model, CLI surface, file format, modes, codebase identity, and integration touchpoints. Read it first. This brief only adds sprint scoping — what's in, what's out, what success looks like.**

## In scope for this sprint

Everything required to make tickets usable end-to-end in both **local-only mode** and **cloud-configured mode**:

1. **Schema migration (cloud-only)**: add `tickets` and `ticket_epics` tables; add `root_commit_sha` column to `codebases` with an index. Additive only — no changes to existing tables beyond the new column.
2. **`megaplan/store/db.py`**: CRUD helpers for the new tables, column allowlist entries, codebase resolution that prefers `root_commit_sha`.
3. **New module `megaplan/tickets/`**: the canonical local representation. Frontmatter read/write for `.megaplan/tickets/{ulid}-{slug}.md` files. Mode-aware dispatch (cloud-configured → file + DB; local-only → file only). Codebase identity resolution from cwd via `git rev-list --max-parents=0 HEAD`.
4. **CLI `megaplan ticket …`** with all verbs in `docs/tickets.md` § CLI: `new`, `list`, `show`, `edit`, `link`, `unlink`, `addressed`, `dismiss`, `reopen`. `ticket new` prints only the ULID to stdout on success; `--json` on read commands. `source` auto-derived from `MEGAPLAN_TURN_ID` env.
5. **Auto-address hook**: in the epic state-transition path, after an epic moves to `done`, call `megaplan.tickets.address_resolved_by_epic(epic_id)`. Implementation handles both modes per the design doc. Idempotent.
6. **Planner discovery integration**: when assembling context for new/refined epics, include open tickets for the current repo, ranked by `tag overlap with epic goal desc, created_at desc`. The planner can propose links with `resolves_on_complete=true`. Same data shape in both modes (DB query in cloud, file scan in local-only).
7. **Resident/agent prompt**: a short paragraph teaching the agent that `megaplan ticket new` exists and when to use it (out-of-scope observations during epic work, user-requested captures).
8. **Tests** covering both modes for: file ↔ DB round-trip, link/unlink, auto-address idempotence, codebase identity resolution (rename case, fresh-repo case), CLI happy paths.

## Out of scope (defer)

- **Offline queue / `.pending/` directory / `megaplan ticket sync`**. Write-through only for now; if the DB write fails in cloud mode, the CLI errors loudly and the user retries. The design doc describes the offline queue — explicitly **do not build it** this sprint. Build the minimal write-through path.
- Conflict detection on concurrent edits — last-write-wins is fine.
- Severity, assignment, comments, cross-repo tickets — explicitly excluded per the design doc.
- Anything described in the doc's **Open items** section.

## Success criteria

- `megaplan ticket new "title" -b "body"` works in a local-only repo and writes the `.md` file.
- The same command in a cloud-configured repo writes the `.md` file **and** inserts a `tickets` row, auto-registering the codebase if needed (with `root_commit_sha` populated).
- `megaplan ticket link <ticket> <epic> --resolves` updates both the file frontmatter and the DB join row.
- Marking an epic `done` flips the linked ticket's status to `addressed` and writes a `resolution_note` referencing the epic — in both modes.
- New epic creation surfaces open tickets for the repo in the planner's context, ranked.
- Tests pass for both modes.

## Notes for the planner

- Follow the **core-first principle**: `megaplan/tickets/` owns the capability; the DB layer is purely additive and only kicks in when a store is configured. Mode detection follows whatever mechanism megaplan core already uses (presence of a configured store) — do not invent a new one.
- The `tickets` concept is **distinct from `feedback`**. Do not reuse the `feedback` table; do not conflate their lifecycles.
- Vendor flags / FK conventions / migration patterns should match how existing tables are handled in `megaplan/store/db.py`. Read existing CRUD helpers before designing new ones.
- The auto-address hook integration point is the one place where you must touch existing epic state-transition code. Find the existing hook surface (where epic events are emitted on state change) and add the call there — don't invent a parallel transition path.

        Current plan (markdown):
        ## Implementation Plan: Tickets MVP

## Overview
Add a repo-scoped tickets feature per `docs/tickets.md`. Build a new `megaplan/tickets/` module that owns the canonical local representation (`.megaplan/tickets/{ulid}-{slug}.md` with YAML frontmatter), wire mode-aware dispatch into the existing DB store, register a new `megaplan ticket …` CLI subcommand group, and inject one auto-address call into the existing epic state-transition path (`megaplan/store/db.py:960` and `megaplan/store/file.py:1054`). DB changes are additive: new `tickets` and `ticket_epics` tables plus a `root_commit_sha` column on `codebases`. Local-only mode operates entirely on files; cloud-configured mode write-throughs to DB. **No offline `.pending/` queue this sprint** — DB write failure raises loudly.

Existing repo shape:
- Migrations live in `supabase/migrations/` (latest: `202605060001_resident_orchestration.sql`). New file should follow naming `2026MMDDHHMM_*.sql`.
- DB store: `megaplan/store/db.py` with column allowlists at line ~237; codebase CRUD already exists (`create_codebase`, `find_codebase`, `update_codebase`, lines 1779–1955).
- CLI: subparsers added in `megaplan/cli.py:1176+`.
- Epic completion sites: `megaplan/store/db.py:960` (DB) and `megaplan/store/file.py:1054` (file backend) — both gate on `changes.get("status") == "done"`. This is the auto-address hook surface.
- Planner context assembly: `megaplan/handlers/plan.py` (and supporting `megaplan/_core/`).

## Main Phase

### Step 1: Audit existing patterns (`megaplan/store/db.py`, `megaplan/store/file.py`, `megaplan/cli.py`, `megaplan/handlers/plan.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Read** the codebase CRUD block (`megaplan/store/db.py:1779-1955`) and column allowlists (`megaplan/store/db.py:237-238`) to mirror naming/return conventions for the new `tickets` / `ticket_epics` helpers.
2. **Read** the epic→done branches at `megaplan/store/db.py:960` and `megaplan/store/file.py:1054` — these are the two call sites for the auto-address hook (one per backend).
3. **Skim** `megaplan/cli.py` ~line 1256 (`epic` subparser) for the established subparser pattern; mirror it for `ticket`.
4. **Locate** where planner context for a new/refined epic is assembled (`megaplan/handlers/plan.py`) and identify the insertion point for "open tickets for repo".

### Step 2: Schema migration (`supabase/migrations/2026MMDDHHMM_tickets.sql`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Create** a new migration file, additive only:
   - `ALTER TABLE codebases ADD COLUMN root_commit_sha text;` + `CREATE INDEX idx_codebases_root_commit_sha ON codebases(root_commit_sha);`
   - `CREATE TABLE tickets (id text PRIMARY KEY, codebase_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES codebases(id), title text NOT NULL, body text NOT NULL, tags text[] DEFAULT '{}', status text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'open' CHECK (status IN ('open','addressed','dismissed')), source text NOT NULL CHECK (source IN ('human','agent','post-mortem')), filed_by_actor_id text REFERENCES … , filed_in_turn_id text REFERENCES bot_turns(id), resolution_note text, created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(), last_edited_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(), addressed_at timestamptz, dismissed_at timestamptz);` plus an index on `(codebase_id, status)`.
   - `CREATE TABLE ticket_epics (ticket_id text REFERENCES tickets(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, epic_id text REFERENCES … , resolves_on_complete boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false, linked_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(), linked_by_actor_id text, note text, PRIMARY KEY (ticket_id, epic_id));` plus an index on `epic_id`.
2. **Match** the FK style (uuid vs text, ON DELETE behavior, `now()` defaults) used by the latest existing migrations — verify by reading `202604300009_009_codebase_research.sql` or similar for actor / bot_turn FK column types.

### Step 3: Schema dataclasses (`megaplan/schemas/models.py` or `megaplan/schemas/sprint1.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `Ticket` and `TicketEpicLink` Pydantic/dataclass models matching the new tables; export from `megaplan/schemas/__init__.py`.
2. **Add** `root_commit_sha: str | None` to the existing `Codebase` model.

### Step 4: DB store CRUD (`megaplan/store/db.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** to the `_IDEMPOTENT_MUTATORS` set and module exports: `create_ticket`, `update_ticket`, `link_ticket_to_epic`, `unlink_ticket_from_epic`, `address_tickets_resolved_by_epic`.
2. **Extend** the column allowlists (`megaplan/store/db.py:237`): add entries for `tickets` and `ticket_epics`; add `root_commit_sha` to the `codebases` allowlist.
3. **Implement** CRUD methods modeled on the codebase block (`megaplan/store/db.py:1779-1955`):
   - `create_ticket(...)`, `load_ticket(id)`, `list_tickets(codebase_id=…, status=…, tag=…, epic_id=…)`, `update_ticket(id, **changes)`.
   - `link_ticket_to_epic(ticket_id, epic_id, resolves_on_complete, note=None)`, `unlink_ticket_from_epic(...)`, `list_ticket_epic_links(ticket_id=…|epic_id=…)`.
   - `address_tickets_resolved_by_epic(epic_id) -> list[str]`: single SQL update setting `status='addressed'`, `addressed_at=now()`, `resolution_note='Resolved by epic <epic_id> on <date>: <title>'` for tickets joined via `ticket_epics` with `resolves_on_complete=true` and current `status='open'`. Returns affected ticket IDs. Idempotent.
4. **Add** codebase resolution helper `resolve_codebase_by_root_sha(root_sha, owner, name, repo_url) -> codebase_id`: lookup by `root_commit_sha` first, fall back to `(owner,name)` and backfill `root_commit_sha`, else `create_codebase` with all four populated.

### Step 5: New module `megaplan/tickets/` (canonical local representation)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Create** `megaplan/tickets/__init__.py` exporting the public API: `new`, `list_open`, `show`, `edit`, `link`, `unlink`, `mark_addressed`, `mark_dismissed`, `reopen`, `address_resolved_by_epic`.
2. **Create** `megaplan/tickets/identity.py`:
   - `repo_root_sha(cwd) -> str` via `git rev-list --max-parents=0 HEAD`; raises a clean error if there are no commits; if multiple roots, sorts and picks smallest.
   - `repo_owner_name(cwd) -> tuple[str|None, str]` from `git remote get-url origin` (best-effort; tolerate missing remote in local-only mode).
3. **Create** `megaplan/tickets/files.py`:
   - `tickets_dir(repo_root) -> Path` returning `<repo>/.megaplan/tickets/`.
   - `read_ticket_file(path) -> TicketDoc` and `write_ticket_file(path, doc)` using a YAML frontmatter parser (use stdlib + a minimal YAML — check if `pyyaml` is already a dep; otherwise use the project's existing frontmatter helper if any in `megaplan/_core/io.py`).
   - `slugify(title)` for filename stem; full path `{ulid}-{slug}.md`.
   - `iter_ticket_files(repo_root)` and `find_ticket_file(repo_root, ulid)`.
4. **Create** `megaplan/tickets/core.py` — mode-aware dispatch:
   - `_get_store()` returns the configured store (mirror whatever `_core` already does for store detection — do not invent a new mechanism).
   - Each public op: always touches the file; if a store is configured, also calls the corresponding DB helper. On DB error in cloud mode: raise loudly (no `.pending/` queue this sprint).
   - `address_resolved_by_epic(epic_id, *, store=None, repo_root=None)`: file-update path always (walk `.megaplan/tickets/*.md`, flip frontmatter `status` to `addressed` for any file whose `epics:` list contains `epic_id` with `resolves_on_complete: true`); cloud path additionally calls `store.address_tickets_resolved_by_epic(epic_id)`. Idempotent: skip files whose status is already `addressed` or `dismissed`.
5. **Source derivation**: helper that reads `MEGAPLAN_TURN_ID` env; if set, populates `source='agent'` + `filed_in_turn`; else `source='human'`.

### Step 6: CLI subcommand group (`megaplan/cli.py`, `megaplan/handlers/tickets.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Register** a new `ticket` subparser in `megaplan/cli.py` (mirror the `epic` block at ~line 1256) with verbs: `new`, `list`, `show`, `edit`, `link`, `unlink`, `addressed`, `dismiss`, `reopen`. Add `--json` to read commands, `--tag/--status/--epic` filters to `list`, `--resolves` to `link`, `--note` to `addressed/dismiss`, `-b/--edit/-` to `new`.
2. **Create** `megaplan/handlers/tickets.py` with one handler per verb that calls into `megaplan.tickets` and renders output.
   - `ticket new`: writes only the ULID to stdout; everything else to stderr.
   - `ticket list`: local-only walks files in cwd; cloud mode queries DB (accepts `--codebase owner/name`).
3. **Wire** dispatch in the main `cli.py` command router.

### Step 7: Auto-address hook (`megaplan/store/db.py:960`, `megaplan/store/file.py:1054`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **In** `megaplan/store/db.py` `update_epic` — after the `status == "done"` branch sets `completed_at`, after the row is persisted, call `megaplan.tickets.address_resolved_by_epic(epic_id, store=self)`. Wrap import locally to avoid circular imports.
2. **Mirror** the same call in `megaplan/store/file.py:1054`.
3. **Idempotency** comes from the helper itself; no extra guards needed here.

### Step 8: Planner discovery integration (`megaplan/handlers/plan.py` + planner prompt assembly)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Identify** the function that builds context for a new/refined epic (in `megaplan/handlers/plan.py` or `megaplan/prompts/`).
2. **Add** a section "Open tickets for this repo" listing the top N (e.g., 20) open tickets ranked by `tag overlap with epic goal/title desc, created_at desc`. Source: cloud mode → `store.list_tickets(codebase_id=…, status='open')`; local-only → `megaplan.tickets.list_open(repo_root)`.
3. **Mention** in the prompt that the planner can propose links with `resolves_on_complete=true` (the actual linking still happens via `ticket link` after epic creation).

### Step 9: Resident/agent prompt (`megaplan/data/instructions.md` or `megaplan/resident/profile.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** a short paragraph to the resident agent instructions: `megaplan ticket new "<title>" -b "<body>"` exists; use it to capture out-of-scope observations during epic work or to record user-requested bugs/notes. ULID is printed to stdout.

### Step 10: Tests (`tests/test_tickets.py`, extend `tests/test_cli_entry.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Local-only mode**: tmp git repo, no store configured. Cover `new` writes the file with correct frontmatter; `list` finds it; `link`/`unlink` updates frontmatter `epics:`; `addressed`/`reopen`/`dismiss` flip `status`; `address_resolved_by_epic` walks files and updates idempotently.
2. **Cloud-configured mode**: with an in-memory/sqlite-backed `DBStore` fixture (mirror existing `tests/conftest.py` patterns). Cover the same ops doing file + DB write-through; auto-register codebase on first call with `root_commit_sha`; `link` writes both file frontmatter and `ticket_epics` row.
3. **Auto-address**: epic transitions to `done` via `update_epic`; verify linked tickets flip to `addressed` in both file and DB; second call is a no-op.
4. **Codebase identity**: rename case (owner/name changes but `root_commit_sha` stable → same `codebase_id`); fresh-repo case (no commits → CLI errors cleanly).
5. **CLI happy paths**: `megaplan ticket new`, `list --json`, `link --resolves` round-trip via `subprocess` or the existing CLI test harness.

### Step 11: Validation
**Scope:** Small
1. **Run** the new test file first: `pytest tests/test_tickets.py -x`.
2. **Run** the broader suite focused on touched areas: `pytest tests/test_cli_entry.py tests/test_auto.py -x`.
3. **Run** the full suite: `pytest -x`.
4. **Smoke**: in a scratch git repo, `MEGAPLAN_TURN_ID=t_test megaplan ticket new "test" -b "body" -t demo`; verify stdout is just the ULID, file exists with `source: agent`, `filed_in_turn: t_test`.

## Execution Order
1. Migration + schema models (Steps 2–3) — defines the data shape everything else builds on.
2. DB CRUD (Step 4) — add and unit-test in isolation.
3. `megaplan/tickets/` module (Step 5) — file path first, then mode dispatch.
4. CLI (Step 6) — exercises the module end-to-end.
5. Auto-address hook (Step 7).
6. Planner integration + agent prompt (Steps 8–9).
7. Tests + validation (Steps 10–11).

## Validation Order
1. Targeted ticket tests (Step 11.1).
2. CLI + epic-transition regression tests (Step 11.2).
3. Full suite (Step 11.3).
4. Manual smoke (Step 11.4).

  "signals": {
    "iteration": 1,
    "idea": "# Sprint brief \u2014 Tickets MVP\n\n## Goal\n\nImplement the tickets feature in megaplan per the design in `docs/tickets.md`. Tickets are short, repo-scoped notes on issues/problems, captured by humans or by agents on a human's prompt, that get folded into epics and auto-addressed when the resolving epic completes.\n\n**The design doc `docs/tickets.md` is the source of truth for the data model, CLI surface, file format, modes, codebase identity, and integration touchpoints. Read it first. This brief only adds sprint scoping \u2014 what's in, what's out, what success looks like.**\n\n## In scope for this sprint\n\nEverything required to make tickets usable end-to-end in both **local-only mode** and **cloud-configured mode**:\n\n1. **Schema migration (cloud-only)**: add `tickets` and `ticket_epics` tables; add `root_commit_sha` column to `codebases` with an index. Additive only \u2014 no changes to existing tables beyond the new column.\n2. **`megaplan/store/db.py`**: CRUD helpers for the new tables, column allowlist entries, codebase resolution that prefers `root_commit_sha`.\n3. **New module `megaplan/tickets/`**: the canonical local representation. Frontmatter read/write for `.megaplan/tickets/{ulid}-{slug}.md` files. Mode-aware dispatch (cloud-configured \u2192 file + DB; local-only \u2192 file only). Codebase identity resolution from cwd via `git rev-list --max-parents=0 HEAD`.\n4. **CLI `megaplan ticket \u2026`** with all verbs in `docs/tickets.md` \u00a7 CLI: `new`, `list`, `show`, `edit`, `link`, `unlink`, `addressed`, `dismiss`, `reopen`. `ticket new` prints only the ULID to stdout on success; `--json` on read commands. `source` auto-derived from `MEGAPLAN_TURN_ID` env.\n5. **Auto-address hook**: in the epic state-transition path, after an epic moves to `done`, call `megaplan.tickets.address_resolved_by_epic(epic_id)`. Implementation handles both modes per the design doc. Idempotent.\n6. **Planner discovery integration**: when assembling context for new/refined epics, include open tickets for the current repo, ranked by `tag overlap with epic goal desc, created_at desc`. The planner can propose links with `resolves_on_complete=true`. Same data shape in both modes (DB query in cloud, file scan in local-only).\n7. **Resident/agent prompt**: a short paragraph teaching the agent that `megaplan ticket new` exists and when to use it (out-of-scope observations during epic work, user-requested captures).\n8. **Tests** covering both modes for: file \u2194 DB round-trip, link/unlink, auto-address idempotence, codebase identity resolution (rename case, fresh-repo case), CLI happy paths.\n\n## Out of scope (defer)\n\n- **Offline queue / `.pending/` directory / `megaplan ticket sync`**. Write-through only for now; if the DB write fails in cloud mode, the CLI errors loudly and the user retries. The design doc describes the offline queue \u2014 explicitly **do not build it** this sprint. Build the minimal write-through path.\n- Conflict detection on concurrent edits \u2014 last-write-wins is fine.\n- Severity, assignment, comments, cross-repo tickets \u2014 explicitly excluded per the design doc.\n- Anything described in the doc's **Open items** section.\n\n## Success criteria\n\n- `megaplan ticket new \"title\" -b \"body\"` works in a local-only repo and writes the `.md` file.\n- The same command in a cloud-configured repo writes the `.md` file **and** inserts a `tickets` row, auto-registering the codebase if needed (with `root_commit_sha` populated).\n- `megaplan ticket link <ticket> <epic> --resolves` updates both the file frontmatter and the DB join row.\n- Marking an epic `done` flips the linked ticket's status to `addressed` and writes a `resolution_note` referencing the epic \u2014 in both modes.\n- New epic creation surfaces open tickets for the repo in the planner's context, ranked.\n- Tests pass for both modes.\n\n## Notes for the planner\n\n- Follow the **core-first principle**: `megaplan/tickets/` owns the capability; the DB layer is purely additive and only kicks in when a store is configured. Mode detection follows whatever mechanism megaplan core already uses (presence of a configured store) \u2014 do not invent a new one.\n- The `tickets` concept is **distinct from `feedback`**. Do not reuse the `feedback` table; do not conflate their lifecycles.\n- Vendor flags / FK conventions / migration patterns should match how existing tables are handled in `megaplan/store/db.py`. Read existing CRUD helpers before designing new ones.\n- The auto-address hook integration point is the one place where you must touch existing epic state-transition code. Find the existing hook surface (where epic events are emitted on state change) and add the call there \u2014 don't invent a parallel transition path.",

        - Each success criterion should include a `requires` field listing the capabilities needed for verification. Valid capability strings: `run_shell`, `read_files`, `run_tests`, `parse_diff`, `read_build_output`, `run_linter` (container), `drive_browser`, `inspect_runtime_ui`, `observe_runtime_logs`, `subjective_judgment`, `verify_physical_device` (human). `must` criteria MUST have non-empty `requires`. Example: `{"criterion": "All tests pass", "priority": "must", "requires": ["run_tests"]}`.
        - Verify that the plan remains aligned with the user's original intent, not just internal plan quality.
        - Remove unjustified scope growth. If critique raised scope creep, narrow the plan back to the original idea unless the broader work is strictly required.
        - Maintain the structural template: H1 title, ## Overview, phase sections with numbered step sections, ## Execution Order or ## Validation Order.
        - CRITICAL: Your entire revised plan markdown (all sections) must be output as the `plan` field in the structured output. The prose response must not contain the plan text.
        - CRITICAL: Return only the structured JSON object for the schema fields `plan`, `changes_summary`, `flags_addressed`, `assumptions`, `success_criteria`, and `questions`. Do not add commentary before or after the JSON object.

        Plan template — simple format (adapt to the actual repo and scope):
````md
# Implementation Plan: [Title]

## Overview
Summarize the goal, current repository shape, and the constraints that matter.

## Main Phase

### Step 1: Audit the current behavior (`megaplan/prompts.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Inspect** the current implementation and call out the exact insertion points (`megaplan/prompts.py:29`).

### Step 2: Add the first change (`megaplan/evaluation.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Implement** the smallest viable change with exact file references (`megaplan/evaluation.py:1`).
2. **Capture** any tricky behavior with a short example.
   ```python
   issues = validate_plan_structure(plan_text)
   ```

### Step 3: Wire downstream behavior (`megaplan/handlers.py`, `megaplan/workers.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Update** the runtime flow in the touched files (`megaplan/handlers.py:400`, `megaplan/workers.py:199`).

### Step 4: Prove the change (`tests/test_evaluation.py`, `tests/test_megaplan.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Run** the cheapest targeted checks first (`tests/test_evaluation.py:1`).
2. **Finish** with broader verification once the wiring is in place (`tests/test_megaplan.py:1`).

## Execution Order
1. Update prompts and mocks before enforcing stricter validation.
2. Land higher-risk wiring after the validator and tests are ready.

## Validation Order
1. Start with focused unit tests.
2. Run the broader suite after the flow changes are in place.
````

For complex plans, use multiple phases:
````md
## Phase 1: Foundation — Dependencies, DB, Types

### Step 1: Install dependencies (`package.json`)
...

### Step 2: Create database migration (`supabase/migrations/`)
...

## Phase 2: Core Integration

### Step 3: Port the main component (`src/components/`)
...
````

Template guidance:
- Simple plans: use `## Main Phase` with `### Step N:` sections underneath.
- Complex plans: use multiple `## Phase N:` sections, each containing `### Step N:` steps. Step numbers are global (not per-phase).
- The flat `## Step N:` format (without phases) also works for backwards compatibility.
- Key invariants: one H1 title, one `## Overview`, numbered step sections (`### Step N:` or `## Step N:`), and at least one ordering section.

Build a new `megaplan/tickets/` module that owns the canonical local representation (`.megaplan/tickets/{ulid}-{slug}.md` with YAML frontmatter), wire mode-aware dispatch into the DB store, register a `megaplan ticket …` CLI subcommand group, and inject one auto-address call into the place where epic state actually transitions to `done`. DB changes are additive: new `tickets` and `ticket_epics` tables plus a `root_commit_sha` column on `codebases`. **No `.pending/` queue this sprint** — DB write failure raises loudly.\n\nVerified repo facts (corrections from prior plan):\n- `update_epic` (`megaplan/store/db.py:584-614`, `megaplan/store/file.py:729-744`) is a **generic UPDATE on arbitrary `**changes`** — there is **no `state == \"done\"` branch today**. The `:960`/`:1054` lines from the previous plan were inside `update_checklist_item` and are wrong.\n- The Epic state column is `state` (not `status`) — see `db.py:627` `state != 'archived'`.\n- Epic state transitions are recorded via `store.record_epic_event` (`db.py:2295`, called from `megaplan/editorial/_events.py:63` and `megaplan/editorial/gating.py:213`). Editorial gating is the canonical surface that drives epics to terminal states.\n- `create_codebase`/`upsert_codebase` INSERT statements (`db.py:1797-1811`, `db.py:1830+`) hard-enumerate columns and **must** be extended to include `root_commit_sha`; allowlist alone is insufficient.\n- There is **no `cloud_configured`/`has_store` predicate** in `_core` today. Both `DBStore` (`db.py:309`) and `FileStore` (`file.py:203`) are valid `Store` implementations. We'll add and use the explicit predicate `megaplan.tickets.is_cloud_store(store) -> bool` defined as `isinstance(store, DBStore)` (single canonical signal, documented in the module).\n- `MEGAPLAN_TURN_ID` is **not currently exported** by the agent runtime (`megaplan/resident/runtime.py` has `turn.id` available locally but does not export it). The agent loop must be updated to set `MEGAPLAN_TURN_ID=<turn.id>` in the env of any subprocess it spawns; otherwise agent-filed tickets misclassify as `human`.\n\n## Phase 1: Schema + Types\n\n### Step 1: Audit existing patterns (`megaplan/store/db.py`, `megaplan/store/file.py`, `megaplan/cli.py`, `megaplan/handlers/plan.py`, `megaplan/editorial/_events.py`, `megaplan/editorial/gating.py`, `megaplan/resident/runtime.py`)\n**Scope:** Small\n1. **Read** the codebase CRUD block (`megaplan/store/db.py:1779-1955`) and column allowlists (`megaplan/store/db.py:237-238`) to mirror naming/return conventions.\n2. **Map** every code path that moves an epic to `state='done'`: `update_epic` (`db.py:584`, `file.py:729`), `record_epic_event` (`db.py:2295`), and the editorial gating call sites (`megaplan/editorial/gating.py:213`, `megaplan/editorial/_events.py:63`). Confirm whether `update_epic` is the bottleneck for state changes or whether some paths bypass it (raw SQL, multi/compat adapters in `megaplan/store/multi.py`, `megaplan/store/compat.py`).\n3. **Locate** `MEGAPLAN_TURN_ID` (currently absent) and the resident agent's subprocess-spawn site in `megaplan/resident/runtime.py` (~line 167) where `turn.id` is in scope.\n4. **Skim** `megaplan/cli.py:1256` (`epic` subparser) for the established subparser pattern.\n5. **Locate** the planner context-assembly site in `megaplan/handlers/plan.py` for the \"open tickets\" section.\n\n### Step 2: Schema migration (`supabase/migrations/2026MMDDHHMM_tickets.sql`)\n**Scope:** Small\n1. **Create** an additive migration:\n   - `ALTER TABLE codebases ADD COLUMN root_commit_sha text;` + `CREATE INDEX idx_codebases_root_commit_sha ON codebases(root_commit_sha);`\n   - `CREATE TABLE tickets (id text PRIMARY KEY, codebase_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES codebases(id), title text NOT NULL CHECK (char_length(title) <= 120), body text NOT NULL, tags text[] NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}', status text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'open' CHECK (status IN ('open','addressed','dismissed')), source text NOT NULL CHECK (source IN ('human','agent','post-mortem')), filed_by_actor_id text REFERENCES automation_actors(id), filed_in_turn_id text REFERENCES bot_turns(id), resolution_note text, created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(), last_edited_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(), addressed_at timestamptz, dismissed_at timestamptz);` + index on `(codebase_id, status)`.\n   - `CREATE TABLE ticket_epics (ticket_id text NOT NULL REFERENCES tickets(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, epic_id text NOT NULL REFERENCES epics(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, resolves_on_complete boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false, linked_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(), linked_by_actor_id text REFERENCES automation_actors(id), note text, PRIMARY KEY (ticket_id, epic_id));` + index on `epic_id`.\n2. **FK column types are explicitly `text`** to match `automation_actors.id`, `bot_turns.id`, and `epics.id` (existing convention). `codebases.id` remains `uuid`.\n\n### Step 3: Schema dataclasses (`megaplan/schemas/models.py`, `megaplan/schemas/__init__.py`)\n**Scope:** Small\n1. **Add** `Ticket` and `TicketEpicLink` Pydantic/dataclass models matching the new tables; export from `megaplan/schemas/__init__.py`.\n2. **Add** `root_commit_sha: str | None = None` to the existing `Codebase` model.\n\n## Phase 2: DB Store\n\n### Step 4: DB store CRUD (`megaplan/store/db.py`)\n**Scope:** Medium\n1. **Extend** `_IDEMPOTENT_MUTATORS` (`db.py:89`) and the module exports list (`db.py:114`) with: `create_ticket`, `update_ticket`, `link_ticket_to_epic`, `unlink_ticket_from_epic`, `address_tickets_resolved_by_epic`, `resolve_codebase_by_root_sha`.\n2. **Extend** the column allowlists (`db.py:237-238`):\n   - Add `root_commit_sha` to the `codebases` entry.\n   - Add `tickets` entry: `{\"id\",\"codebase_id\",\"title\",\"body\",\"tags\",\"status\",\"source\",\"filed_by_actor_id\",\"filed_in_turn_id\",\"resolution_note\",\"created_at\",\"last_edited_at\",\"addressed_at\",\"dismissed_at\"}`.\n   - Add `ticket_epics` entry: `{\"ticket_id\",\"epic_id\",\"resolves_on_complete\",\"linked_at\",\"linked_by_actor_id\",\"note\"}`.\n3. **Extend `create_codebase` and `upsert_codebase` INSERT SQL** (`db.py:1797-1811`, `db.py:1830+`) to add `root_commit_sha` to the column list, the `VALUES` placeholders, and the parameter list. Add `root_commit_sha: str | None = None` to both signatures. For `upsert_codebase`, also add it to the `ON CONFLICT … DO UPDATE SET` clause so re-upsert backfills it. **Do not modify any other column behavior.**\n4. **Implement** new helpers modeled on the codebase block:\n   - `create_ticket(...)`, `load_ticket(id)`, `list_tickets(codebase_id=…, status=…, tag=…, epic_id=…)`, `update_ticket(id, *, expected_revision=None, **changes)` (revision optional — tickets table has no revision column; last-write-wins per the brief).\n   - `link_ticket_to_epic(ticket_id, epic_id, *, resolves_on_complete=False, linked_by_actor_id=None, note=None)` — INSERT … ON CONFLICT (ticket_id, epic_id) DO UPDATE SET `resolves_on_complete = EXCLUDED.resolves_on_complete`, `note = EXCLUDED.note` (idempotent re-link).\n   - `unlink_ticket_from_epic(ticket_id, epic_id)`, `list_ticket_epic_links(ticket_id=…|epic_id=…)`.\n   - `address_tickets_resolved_by_epic(epic_id) -> list[str]`: single SQL `UPDATE tickets SET status='addressed', addressed_at=now(), resolution_note=$2 FROM ticket_epics te JOIN epics e ON e.id=te.epic_id WHERE te.ticket_id=tickets.id AND te.epic_id=$1 AND te.resolves_on_complete=true AND tickets.status='open' RETURNING tickets.id`. The `resolution_note` is built as `f\"Resolved by epic {epic_id} on {date}: {epic.title}\"` with epic title fetched first. Returns affected ticket IDs. Idempotent (the `status='open'` filter makes re-runs no-ops).\n5. **`resolve_codebase_by_root_sha(*, root_sha, owner, name, repo_url, default_branch) -> str`** (returns codebase_id):\n   - SELECT by `root_commit_sha = root_sha`. If found and `(owner,name)` differ, silently `UPDATE codebases SET owner=…, name=…, repo_url=…` (rename case); return id.\n   - Else SELECT by `(lower(owner), lower(name))`. If found, UPDATE that row to set `root_commit_sha = root_sha` (backfill); return id.\n   - Else `create_codebase(... root_commit_sha=root_sha)` and return id.\n\n## Phase 3: Tickets Module\n\n### Step 5: New module `megaplan/tickets/` (canonical local representation)\n**Scope:** Medium\n1. **Create** `megaplan/tickets/__init__.py` exporting public API: `new`, `list_open`, `show`, `edit`, `link`, `unlink`, `mark_addressed`, `mark_dismissed`, `reopen`, `address_resolved_by_epic`, `is_cloud_store`.\n2. **Create** `megaplan/tickets/identity.py`:\n   - `repo_root_sha(cwd) -> str` via `git rev-list --max-parents=0 HEAD`. If the command exits non-zero or returns empty (no commits), raise `TicketsError(\"No commits in this repository — make your first commit before filing a ticket.\")`. If multiple roots, sort lexically and pick the smallest.\n   - `repo_owner_name(cwd) -> tuple[str|None, str|None]` parsing `git remote get-url origin`; `(None, None)` if no remote (local-only is fine).\n   - `repo_default_branch(cwd) -> str` (best-effort `git symbolic-ref --short HEAD` or `main`).\n3. **Create** `megaplan/tickets/files.py`:\n   - `tickets_dir(repo_root) -> Path` returning `<repo>/.megaplan/tickets/` (mkdir on write).\n   - `read_ticket_file(path) -> TicketDoc`, `write_ticket_file(path, doc)` — YAML frontmatter via `pyyaml` (already a transitive dep; verify in `pyproject.toml` and add explicitly if missing).\n   - `slugify(title)` (ASCII, lowercase, hyphens, ≤40 chars).\n   - `iter_ticket_files(repo_root)`, `find_ticket_file(repo_root, ulid)`.\n4. **Create** `megaplan/tickets/core.py` — mode-aware dispatch:\n   - `is_cloud_store(store) -> bool`: `return isinstance(store, DBStore)`. **This is the canonical mode-detection predicate** — single source of truth for tickets and any future caller.\n   - `_resolve_store() -> Store | None`: returns the configured store from megaplan's existing store factory (read `megaplan/_core/state.py` / `megaplan/store/__init__.py` for the loader; reuse it — do not invent a new factory). Returns `None` when no store is loadable.\n   - Each public op always writes the file first; if `is_cloud_store(store)`, also call the corresponding DB helper. **DB error in cloud mode → raise loudly** (no `.pending/` queue).\n   - `address_resolved_by_epic(epic_id, *, store=None, repo_root=None)`: signature accepts an explicit `store` (the **tickets/cloud store**, not the epic-event caller's `self`); when called from the epic hook (Step 7) the caller passes the result of `_resolve_store()`, not its own backend. File path always: walk `tickets_dir(repo_root)`, flip frontmatter `status` to `addressed` and set `addressed_at` for any ticket whose `epics:` list contains `epic_id` with `resolves_on_complete: true` and current `status == 'open'` (idempotent skip on `addressed`/`dismissed`). Cloud path: additionally call `store.address_tickets_resolved_by_epic(epic_id)`.\n5. **Source derivation** (`megaplan/tickets/core.py`): `_derive_source() -> tuple[str, str|None]` reads `MEGAPLAN_TURN_ID` env. If set: return `('agent', turn_id)`. Else: `('human', None)`. `filed_by_actor_id` is read from `MEGAPLAN_ACTOR_ID` env (set by the same agent path) when present.\n6. **Stdin body**: in `new(...)`, when the CLI passes body=`-`, read `sys.stdin.read()`; reject empty body. The CLI layer (Step 6) is responsible for translating `-b -` → `body=sys.stdin.read()`.\n\n## Phase 4: CLI + Hooks\n\n### Step 6: CLI subcommand group (`megaplan/cli.py`, `megaplan/handlers/tickets.py`)\n**Scope:** Medium\n1. **Register** a `ticket` subparser in `megaplan/cli.py` (mirror `epic` block at ~`cli.py:1256`):\n   - `new <title> [-b BODY | --edit | -] [-t TAG[,TAG…]]` — `--edit` opens `$EDITOR` on a tmp file; `-` reads body from stdin (`sys.stdin.read()`, must be non-empty); `-b` takes inline string. Mutually exclusive group; required (no body → error).\n   - `list [--tag X] [--status open] [--epic E] [--codebase OWNER/NAME] [--json]`\n   - `show <id> [--json]`, `edit <id>` (opens `$EDITOR` on the `.md`)\n   - `link <ticket> <epic> [--resolves]`, `unlink <ticket> <epic>`\n   - `addressed <id> [--note \"...\"]`, `dismiss <id> [--note \"...\"]`, `reopen <id>`\n2. **Create** `megaplan/handlers/tickets.py` with one handler per verb. Handlers call `megaplan.tickets.*`. `ticket new` writes **only the ULID to stdout**; all chatter to stderr.\n3. **Wire** dispatch in `cli.py` command router.\n\n### Step 7: Auto-address hook (`megaplan/store/db.py`, `megaplan/store/file.py`)\n**Scope:** Small\n1. **Hook surface — `update_epic`**: in both `megaplan/store/db.py:584` and `megaplan/store/file.py:729`, after the row is persisted, gate on `changes.get(\"state\") == \"done\"` (epic column is `state`, not `status`). When true, call:\n   ```python\n   from megaplan.tickets.core import address_resolved_by_epic, _resolve_store\n   address_resolved_by_epic(epic_id, store=_resolve_store(), repo_root=os.getcwd())\n   ```\n   The `store` passed is the **resolved tickets/cloud store**, not `self` — this matters because in mixed-backend setups (multi/compat adapters) the caller's `self` may be a non-cloud store while a `DBStore` is configured for tickets.\n2. **Hook surface — `record_epic_event`** (`db.py:2295`): also add the same gated call when the recorded event represents a transition to `state='done'` (check `event_type` and `post_state.get('state')`). Editorial gating drives transitions through this path; without coverage here, gating-driven completions silently miss the hook (per scope-1).\n3. **Audit** `megaplan/store/multi.py` and `megaplan/store/compat.py` for any wrappers around `update_epic`/`record_epic_event` that could rename `changes['state']` or bypass these methods. If found, hook there or normalize the key. Document any path that cannot be hooked as a known limitation.\n4. **Idempotency** is provided by `address_resolved_by_epic` itself (file path skips already-addressed; SQL filters `status='open'`). No extra guard at call sites.\n\n### Step 8: Planner discovery integration (`megaplan/handlers/plan.py`)\n**Scope:** Small\n1. **Identify** the function building context for new/refined epics in `megaplan/handlers/plan.py`.\n2. **Add** an \"Open tickets for this repo\" section (top 20) ranked **strictly per the brief**: `tag overlap with epic goal desc, created_at desc`. Compute overlap as `len(set(ticket.tags) & set(tokens(epic.goal)))` — the source of overlap is the **epic goal only**, not title. Source: cloud → `store.list_tickets(codebase_id, status='open')`; local-only → `megaplan.tickets.list_open(repo_root)`.\n3. **Mention** in the prompt that the planner can propose links with `resolves_on_complete=true`; actual linking still happens via `megaplan ticket link --resolves` after epic creation.\n\n### Step 9: Resident/agent wiring + prompt (`megaplan/resident/runtime.py`, `megaplan/data/instructions.md`)\n**Scope:** Small\n1. **Export** `MEGAPLAN_TURN_ID=<turn.id>` (and `MEGAPLAN_ACTOR_ID` if available) in the env of any subprocess the resident agent spawns — locate the spawn site in `megaplan/resident/runtime.py` (~line 167 where `turn.id` is in scope) and add to its env dict. Without this, agent-filed tickets misclassify as `human`.\n2. **Add** to `megaplan/data/instructions.md`: a short paragraph that `megaplan ticket new \"<title>\" -b \"<body>\"` exists; use it to capture out-of-scope observations during epic work or user-requested bugs/notes; ULID is printed to stdout.\n\n## Phase 5: Tests + Validation\n\n### Step 10: Tests (`tests/test_tickets.py`, `tests/test_tickets_cli.py`, extend `tests/test_cli_entry.py`)\n**Scope:** Medium\n1. **Local-only mode** (tmp git repo, no DBStore configured): `new` writes file with frontmatter; `new -b -` reads stdin; `list`/`show`/`edit`; `link`/`unlink` mutates frontmatter `epics:`; `addressed`/`reopen`/`dismiss` flip status; `address_resolved_by_epic` walks files idempotently.\n2. **Cloud-configured mode** (DBStore fixture from `tests/conftest.py`): same ops do file + DB write-through; `link --resolves` writes both file and `ticket_epics` row.\n3. **Codebase auto-registration**: first `ticket new` in a fresh repo creates a `codebases` row with `root_commit_sha` populated (verify the column is **non-NULL** — guards FLAG-002 / all_locations-1).\n4. **Auto-address hook**: drive an epic to `state='done'` via `update_epic` AND via the editorial gating path that calls `record_epic_event`; verify linked tickets flip to `addressed` in both file and DB; second transition is a no-op. Also verify a non-`done` `update_epic` call does **not** trigger the hook.\n5. **Codebase identity**: rename case (owner/name change but same root SHA → same `codebase_id`); fresh-repo case (no commits → CLI errors cleanly with the documented message).\n6. **CLI happy paths** via subprocess: `ticket new` stdout is exactly the ULID + newline (nothing else); `list --json` is valid JSON; `link --resolves` round-trip.\n\n### Step 11: Validation\n**Scope:** Small\n1. `pytest tests/test_tickets.py tests/test_tickets_cli.py -x`.\n2. `pytest tests/test_cli_entry.py tests/test_auto.py -x` (regression on CLI + epic transitions).\n3. `pytest -x` (full suite).\n4. **Smoke**: in a scratch git repo, `MEGAPLAN_TURN_ID=t_test megaplan ticket new \"test\" -b \"body\" -t demo`; verify stdout is just the ULID; file has `source: agent`, `filed_in_turn: t_test`.\n\n## Execution Order\n1. Phase 1 (migration + models) — defines data shape.\n2. Phase 2 (DB CRUD + extended `create_codebase` INSERT).\n3. Phase 3 (`megaplan/tickets/` module with `is_cloud_store` predicate).\n4. Phase 4: CLI (Step 6) → auto-address hook in `update_epic` AND `record_epic_event` (Step 7) → planner + agent env (Steps 8–9).\n5. Phase 5: tests + validation.\n\n## Validation Order\n1. Targeted ticket tests (Step 11.1).\n2. CLI + epic-transition regression (Step 11.2).\n3. Full suite (Step 11.3).\n4. Manual smoke (Step 11.4).",

Body source is a mutually exclusive required group; `-` reads stdin via `sys.stdin.read()` and rejects empty.\n2. `megaplan/handlers/tickets.py` — one handler per verb. `ticket new` writes only ULID + newline to stdout; chatter to stderr.\n3.
